Now I have the SCD fallback training results:


- Training: epoch=2.0, train_loss=0.023, train_runtime=6:58:46
- Final eval: eval_loss=0.0333

The training loss metrics look good, but to actually measure the NER F1 improvement from SCD, I'll need to merge the LoRA adapter and run inference on the test set. The eval loss alone won't show me the real performance gain. Comparing against the CoNLL control run, the fallback's eval_loss of 0.0333 is better than CoNLL's 0.0362, which is a positive sign.

Both SCD experiments are showing very low training loss around 0.02-0.03, indicating the model is learning effectively from the pseudo-label and gold data mixture. The critical question is whether this translates to actual NER F1 gains—the CoNLL control showed a +1.06pp improvement, so if FewNERD shows similar or better results, that would validate SCD as a meaningful contribution. Once the standard training finishes, I'll need to merge both models and run inference to get the final F1 scores.

The standard run is nearly done — just 25 steps away from completion, so the final adapter files and results should appear soon. Meanwhile, the threshold variant is in its final evaluation phase with about 10 minutes left to go. To actually measure whether these training runs improved NER performance, I'll need to merge the LoRA adapters back onto the base model and run inference to compare the F1 scores against the original.

Both the fallback and standard SCD runs are tracking normally with no concerning patterns in their loss curves. Let me pull together a full status snapshot across the cluster to see if there's anything worth flagging to the Director. Now I'm looking at the performance metrics across these experiments—the FewNERD model is at 72% completion, and I've got several key findings to track: the SCD fallback finished successfully, the standard version is nearly done, and the threshold eval is in its final stages. There's an interesting anomaly with CoNLL showing a +1.06pp gain despite negative construction results, likely due to gold data in the training mix. I should wait for the SCD standard to finish in about a minute before posting a comprehensive review.

Actually, I'm realizing I shouldn't hold back—my instructions are clear that I should verify conclusions don't depend on downstream experiments and report issues immediately rather than waiting for everything to complete. The SCD CoNLL control concern is a real problem that could affect the entire narrative, so I should post about that now instead of waiting for all the inference results.

The core issue is that the negative control failed unexpectedly: construction hurt CoNLL by 0.4pp, yet SCD still improved F1 by 1.06pp. This suggests the improvement might not come from learning better constructions, but rather from the additional training data or extra training steps themselves. Since SCD mixes pseudo-labels 1:1 with gold data, I can't isolate whether the gains are actually from the SCD mechanism or just from having more training signal overall—I'd need a control that adds the same amount of gold data without pseudo-labels to know for sure.

This is a significant experimental design flaw I need to bring to the Director's attention. Let me also check whether the standard SCD log has finished running.

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
SCD Standard has completed training! The final training metrics are:
- train_loss: 0.023
- train_runtime: 7:08:09.02
- train_samples_per_second: 5.071
- train_steps_per_second: 0.317

And it's now in the final eval phase at 6% (286/4706). So it's running the final evaluation. ETA ~10 min for eval to complete.

The checkpoint directory shows only checkpoints (4000, 7500, 8000) without the final adapter files, meaning it's still in eval phase.


Now I'm compiling the status update for the Director. The fallback variant finished with an eval loss of 0.0333, the standard variant is wrapping up its evaluation in about 9 minutes, and the threshold variant is similarly in eval at 3%. The CoNLL control surprisingly showed a +1.06 percentage point F1 improvement, which is worth flagging.

The issue is that this control was meant to be negative, but the improvement suggests a confound—the training mixed pseudo-labels with gold data equally, so I can't tell if the gain comes from the pseudo-labels themselves or just from having more gold training data. Without a gold-only baseline using the same training steps, the SCD narrative about distilling construction knowledge into greedy inference gets muddied. I should recommend running that control experiment before claiming SCD as a real contribution.

Actually, wait—let me reconsider. The base model was already fine-tuned on CoNLL, so additional epochs on the same gold data would risk overfitting rather than helping. If the model already converged during the original fine-tuning, more gold epochs shouldn't improve much. And the pseudo-labels come from the test set in a transductive setup. So if gold-only training for the same number of steps doesn't help, that would actually support the SCD improvement as genuine rather than a confound.

But here's the puzzle: on CoNLL, the pseudo-labels were harmful on average (−0.4pp), yet training on them still improved F1. This suggests the model might be benefiting from additional training iterations on the gold data itself, or there's something about the pseudo-label mixture that helps despite their lower quality.

For FewNERD, though, the construction gain was positive (+1.6pp), meaning the pseudo-labels are actually better than greedy outputs. So I can compare the two: if FewNERD shows significantly larger improvements than CoNLL, that points to genuine pseudo-label quality mattering. If both improve similarly, it's more likely just the confound of extra training data.

I need to flag this instability with the Director right now since the CoNLL control failed—my instructions are clear that unstable conclusions require immediate action. I'll document the SCD training status, the CoNLL concern, and recommend adding a gold-only baseline control to isolate the effect.
```

This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   I am acting as an independent Reviewer (独立科学审查员) for the `struct_self_consist_ie` project targeting EMNLP 2026 Main Conference (ARR May cycle, deadline 2026-05-25). My role is to independently verify experiment conclusions, check GPU resource boundaries (L1 red line #4), assess spec completeness, monitor training progress, and report findings to the Director via `reviewer_post_to_director`. All output must be in Chinese. This session continued from a compacted conversation, resuming monitoring of SCD (Self-Consistency Distillation) experiments on autodl-5090-b and tracking all running experiments across 3 GPU servers.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **Self-Consistency Distillation (SCD)**: Fine-tune model on SC pseudo-labels (mixed 1:1 with gold data) to improve greedy performance. 3 FewNERD variants: standard (seed 42), fallback (seed 43), threshold (θ=3/N ablation). Plus CoNLL negative control.
   - **Base model**: All SCD experiments use **Qwen3-8B-FT** (`qwen3-8b-fewnerd-exp021-merged`), confirmed via logs showing `Qwen3Config` / `Qwen3ForCausalLM`
   - **SCD CoNLL Control Surprise**: Intended negative control (construction gain = -0.4pp on CoNLL). Result: **+1.06pp F1 improvement** — negative control NOT confirmed. Raises confound: improvement may come from additional gold training data in the 1:1 mix, not from pseudo-labels.
   - **Transductive SCD setting**: Pseudo-labels generated from test set SC outputs, mixed with train set gold data
   - **LlamaFactory**: Training framework used for all SCD experiments (`llamafactory-cli train`)
   - **GPU boundary enforcement**: L1 red line requiring all GPU usage within project whitelist (cuda:0-3 on all 3 servers)
   - **Micro-F1 standardization**: D204 decision to unify all experiments to micro-F1 metric
   - **32B QLoRA**: Qwen2.5-32B-Instruct training on autodl-5090 with 4-GPU torchrun
   - **14B FewNERD inference**: Running N=2 (95%), kill-after-N=8 script monitoring on autodl-5090-c
   - **T_low inference**: T=0.5, N=8 sampling on FewNERD for heterogeneous-temperature analysis

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - **`/root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/logs/exp_b1_scd_fewnerd_standard.log`** (autodl-5090-b)
     - SCD standard training log. Training completed: train_loss=0.023, runtime=7:08:09, 8142 total steps, epoch=2.0
     - Final eval started, at 6% (286/4706) when last checked
     - Base model: `qwen3-8b-fewnerd-exp021-merged` (Qwen3Config)

   - **`/root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/logs/exp_b1_scd_fewnerd_fallback.log`** (autodl-5090-b)
     - SCD fallback training log. FULLY COMPLETED including eval.
     - Training: epoch=2.0, train_loss=0.023, runtime=6:58:46
     - Mid-training eval at epoch 1.97: eval_loss=0.034
     - Final eval: eval_loss=0.0333, eval_runtime=10:05.54
     - Checkpoint: `qwen3-8b-fewnerd-scd-fallback-lora` with adapter_model.safetensors (349MB)

   - **`/root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/logs/exp_b1_scd_threshold.log`** (autodl-5090-b)
     - SCD threshold training log. Training completed (9412 total steps), entered final eval at 3% (149/4706)
     - Checkpoint saved at step 8000: `qwen3-8b-fewnerd-scd-theta3-lora/checkpoint-8000`

   - **`/root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/logs/exp_b1_scd_conll_control.log`** (autodl-5090-b)
     - SCD CoNLL control. FULLY COMPLETED. Base: `qwen3-8b-conll2003-merged`
     - eval_loss=0.0362, 813 eval steps, 690 training steps
     - Registry result: pre_scd_greedy_f1=0.9078, post_scd_greedy_f1=0.9184, delta=+1.06pp

   - **`/root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/checkpoints/qwen3-8b-fewnerd-scd-fallback-lora/`** (autodl-5090-b)
     - Completed checkpoint directory with: adapter_config.json, adapter_model.safetensors (349MB), all_results.json, eval_results.json, train_results.json, checkpoint-4000, checkpoint-8000, checkpoint-8142

   - **`/root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/checkpoints/qwen3-8b-fewnerd-scd-lora/`** (autodl-5090-b)
     - Standard SCD checkpoint directory — only has checkpoint-4000, checkpoint-7500, checkpoint-8000 and trainer_log.jsonl (no final adapter files yet, eval still running)

   - **`/root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/logs/train_qwen25_32b_fewnerd_s42.log`** (autodl-5090)
     - 32B QLoRA training at 86% (35513/41180), epoch 4.31, loss=0.0062, ~2.63s/step, ~4h remaining

   - **Registry (decisions.yaml)**: D200-D204 checked. D204 addresses micro-F1 standardization but does NOT explicitly address ASB greedy_f1 data leakage or budget allocation bugs. `_next_id: 205`.

   - **all_results.json** (SCD fallback):
     ```json
     {"epoch": 2.0, "eval_loss": 0.03326401859521866, "eval_runtime": 605.542,
      "eval_samples_per_second": 31.086, "eval_steps_per_second": 7.772,

      "train_steps_per_second": 0.324}
     ```

4. Errors and Fixes:
   - **ps aux format error**: `ps aux --no-headers -o pid,etime,cmd --sort=-etime` failed with "conflicting format options". Fixed by using plain `ps aux --no-headers` without -o flags.
   - **grep pipe blocked**: `grep -i "model_name\|model_type\|qwen" ... | head` was blocked by ssh_read_only's shell metacharacter restriction (pipe `|`). Worked around by using grep without pipe.
   - **SCD CoNLL checkpoint path not found**: `ls /root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/checkpoints/qwen3-8b-conll2003-scd-lora/` returned "No such file or directory". This means the CoNLL SCD checkpoint was saved under a different name or the experiment was handled differently.
   - **Log truncation**: Many tail_training_logs calls returned truncated output due to extremely long progress bar output. Managed by adjusting `n` parameter and focusing on the latest lines.

5. Problem Solving:
   - **Model verification**: Successfully confirmed all 3 SCD experiments use Qwen3-8B-FT base model (not Qwen2.5-7B), consistent with registry spec. Initial concern about model mismatch was resolved.
   - **GPU boundary compliance**: Verified all 12 GPUs across 3 servers — all processes within project whitelist. No violations found.
   - **SCD training completion tracking**: Monitored all 3 SCD FewNERD experiments through training completion. Fallback fully done including eval; standard and threshold in final eval phases.
   - **SCD CoNLL control concern identified**: The +1.06pp improvement despite negative construction gain raises a confound question about whether SCD benefit comes from pseudo-labels or additional gold training data. This is a critical interpretive issue for the paper's SCD narrative.
   - **ASB bug status**: Confirmed D204 does not explicitly address the greedy_f1 data leakage bug flagged in previous session. Issue remains unresolved.
   - **exp_e_theta_adaptive stale**: Still marked RUNNING in registry despite having no active process. Flagged in previous session, not yet corrected.

6. All User Messages:
   - "Continue from where you left off." (initial continuation from compacted session, implied by context restoration)
   - "Tool loaded." (after ToolSearch for ssh_read_only and tail_training_logs)
   - "Tool loaded." (after ToolSearch for read_registry)
   - "Tool loaded." (after ToolSearch for read_project_file)
   - "Tool loaded." (after ToolSearch for reviewer_post_to_director)
   - "CRITICAL: Respond with TEXT ONLY. Do NOT call any tools..." (summary request)

7. Pending Tasks:
   - **Post SCD CoNLL control concern to Director**: Was about to call `reviewer_post_to_director` to flag the confound issue when interrupted by summary request. The critical design concern: SCD training mixes pseudo-labels 1:1 with gold data, so the +1.06pp CoNLL improvement cannot be attributed to pseudo-labels vs additional gold training without a gold-only baseline control.
   - **SCD FewNERD inference review**: All 3 SCD FewNERD experiments need LoRA merging + inference to get actual NER F1 numbers. Training eval_loss alone doesn't tell us the prescriptive improvement. Must wait for Agent to merge and run inference.
   - **Monitor SCD standard and threshold eval completion**: Both in final eval phase (~10 min each at time of interruption)
   - **T_low FewNERD completion review**: Was at 72%, should complete soon. Enables heterogeneous-temperature analysis.
   - **14B FewNERD inference tracking**: N=2 at 95%, then N=4 and N=8. Kill-after-N=8 script deployed.
   - **32B QLoRA training completion**: At 86%, ~4h remaining.
   - **ASB re-run verification**: After Agent fixes greedy_f1 data leakage + budget allocation bugs + switches to micro-F1
   - **HumanEval MV metric fix**: `mv_pass_rate` tautology identified in previous session, not yet fixed
   - **exp_e_theta_adaptive status correction**: Should be DONE (negative), still RUNNING in registry

8. Current Work:
   I was actively monitoring the completion of SCD training experiments across autodl-5090-b and was about to post a review to the Director about a critical design concern with the SCD CoNLL control experiment. Specifically:

   **SCD Cluster Status at ~03:00 UTC May 21:**
   - SCD fallback (seed=43, GPU 1): ✅ FULLY DONE — eval_loss=0.0333, checkpoint saved with 349MB adapter
   - SCD standard (seed=42, GPU 0): Training DONE (train_loss=0.023), final eval at 6% (~9 min remaining)
   - SCD threshold (θ=3/N, GPU 3): Training DONE, final eval at 3% (~10 min remaining)
   - GPU 2: IDLE

   **Other Servers:**
   - autodl-5090: 32B QLoRA at 86% (35513/41180), epoch 4.31, loss=0.0062, ~4h ETA
   - autodl-5090-c GPU 0: 14B FewNERD N=2 at 95%, then N=4/N=8 (~8h total)
   - autodl-5090-c GPU 2: T_low FewNERD at 72%, ~13 min remaining

   I had loaded the `reviewer_post_to_director` tool schema and was composing a review about the SCD CoNLL control confound when the summary was requested.

9. Optional Next Step:
   Post the SCD CoNLL control design concern to Director via `reviewer_post_to_director`. The review should flag:
   - [severity: warning] SCD CoNLL control shows +1.06pp improvement despite negative construction gain (-0.4pp)
   - The 1:1 pseudo-label + gold mix creates a confound: cannot distinguish pseudo-label benefit from additional gold training epochs
   - Recommendation: Before claiming SCD as a prescriptive contribution, compare FewNERD SCD improvement magnitude vs CoNLL. If FewNERD improvement >> CoNLL improvement, pseudo-label quality matters (genuine SCD mechanism). If similar, likely confounded.
   - Also flag unresolved issues: ASB bugs not explicitly addressed in D204, exp_e_theta_adaptive still RUNNING

   Then continue monitoring SCD standard and threshold eval completion, and wait for the Agent to merge LoRA adapters and run NER inference to get actual F1 comparisons (pre-SCD greedy F1 vs post-SCD greedy F1).

{
  "parentUuid": "fd65e3df-bd06-4bac-844f-5c40cb25a83b",
  "isSidechain": false,
  "attachment": {
    "type": "file",
    "filename": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_1/projects/struct_self_consist_ie/artifacts/experiments/ace05_re/prepare_re_data.py",
    "content": {
      "type": "text",
      "file": {
        "filePath": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_1/projects/struct_self_consist_ie/artifacts/experiments/ace05_re/prepare_re_data.py",
        "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Prepare SciERC data for Relation Extraction SC experiments.\n\nLoads SciERC test split, filters to instances with ≥1 gold relation,\nbuilds few-shot RE prompts, and outputs JSONL for the sampling pipeline.\n\nUsage:\n    python prepare_re_data.py \\\n        --scierc-dir /path/to/scierc \\\n        --output /path/to/re_test_instances.jsonl \\\n        --few-shot-k 3\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport json\nimport os\nimport random\nimport sys\n\nsys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), \"../..\"))\n\nfrom data_utils import load_scierc, Instance\nfrom sampling import SCIERC_SCHEMA_HINT\n\n\nSCHEMA_HINT = SCIERC_SCHEMA_HINT\n\nRE_FEWSHOT_INSTRUCTION = (\n    \"Extract all relations (head entity, relation type, tail entity) from the given text. \"\n    \"Output a JSON object with fields: entities, relations, events.\\n\\n\"\n    f\"{SCHEMA_HINT}\\n\\n\"\n    \"Output format: \"\n    '{\"entities\": [], '\n    '\"relations\": [{\"head\": \"...\", \"tail\": \"...\", \"type\": \"...\", '\n    '\"head_start\": &lt;int>, \"head_end\": &lt;int>, \"tail_start\": &lt;int>, \"tail_end\": &lt;int>}], '\n    '\"events\": []}\\n'\n)\n\n\ndef instance_to_demo(inst: Instance) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Format a gold instance as a few-shot demonstration.\"\"\"\n    text = inst[\"text\"]\n    gold_rels = inst.get(\"relations\", [])\n    gold_json = json.dumps(\n        {\"entities\": [], \"relations\": gold_rels, \"events\": []},\n        ensure_ascii=False,\n    )\n    return f\"Text: {text}\\nOutput: {gold_json}\"\n\n\ndef build_fewshot_prompt(\n    inst: Instance,\n    demos: list[Instance],\n) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Build a few-shot RE prompt for a test instance.\"\"\"\n    parts = [RE_FEWSHOT_INSTRUCTION]\n    for i, demo in enumerate(demos):\n        parts.append(f\"### Example {i + 1}\\n{instance_to_demo(demo)}\")\n    parts.append(f\"### Now extract:\\nText: {inst['text']}\\nOutput:\")\n    return \"\\n\\n\".join(parts)\n\n\ndef build_zeroshot_prompt(inst: Instance) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Build a zero-shot RE prompt for a test instance.\"\"\"\n    return (\n        f\"{RE_FEWSHOT_INSTRUCTION}\\n\"\n        f\"Text: {inst['text']}\\nOutput:\"\n    )\n\n\ndef select_demos(\n    train_instances: list[Instance],\n    k: int,\n    seed: int = 42,\n) -> list[Instance]:\n    \"\"\"Select k diverse few-shot demonstrations from training set.\n\n    Picks instances that have relations AND cover diverse relation types.\n    \"\"\"\n    rel_instances = [inst for inst in train_instances if inst.get(\"relations\")]\n    if not rel_instances:\n        return []\n\n    rng = random.Random(seed)\n\n    seen_types: set[str] = set()\n    selected: list[Instance] = []\n\n    rng.shuffle(rel_instances)\n    for inst in rel_instances:\n        rel_types = {r[\"type\"] for r in inst[\"relations\"]}\n        new_types = rel_types - seen_types\n        if new_types:\n            selected.append(inst)\n            seen_types |= rel_types\n            if len(selected) >= k:\n                break\n\n    while len(selected) < k and len(selected) < len(rel_instances):\n        candidate = rng.choice(rel_instances)\n        if candidate not in selected:\n            selected.append(candidate)\n\n    return selected[:k]\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Prepare SciERC RE data for SC experiments\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--scierc-dir\", required=True, help=\"Path to SciERC data directory\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--output\", required=True, help=\"Output JSONL path for test instances\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--demo-output\", default=None, help=\"Output path for few-shot demos JSON\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--few-shot-k\", type=int, default=3, help=\"Number of few-shot examples\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--prompt-mode\", choices=[\"zeroshot\", \"fewshot\", \"train_aligned\"],\n                        default=\"fewshot\", help=\"Prompt construction mode\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--min-relations\", type=int, default=1,\n                        help=\"Minimum gold relations to include instance\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--seed\", type=int, default=42)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--stats\", action=\"store_true\", help=\"Print dataset statistics\")\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    splits = load_scierc(args.scierc_dir)\n    train_all = splits[\"train\"]\n    test_all = splits[\"test\"]\n\n    test_re = [inst for inst in test_all if len(inst.get(\"relations\", [])) >= args.min_relations]\n\n    if args.stats:\n        for split_name, insts in [(\"train\", train_all), (\"test\", test_all), (\"test_re_filtered\", test_re)]:\n            n_rels = sum(len(inst.get(\"relations\", [])) for inst in insts)\n            n_with_rel = sum(1 for inst in insts if inst.get(\"relations\"))\n            rel_types = set()\n            for inst in insts:\n                for r in inst.get(\"relations\", []):\n                    rel_types.add(r[\"type\"])\n            print(f\"{split_name}: {len(insts)} instances, {n_with_rel} with relations, \"\n                  f\"{n_rels} total relations, types: {sorted(rel_types)}\")\n\n    demos = select_demos(train_all, args.few_shot_k, seed=args.seed)\n\n    output_instances = []\n    for inst in test_re:\n        if args.prompt_mode == \"fewshot\":\n            prompt = build_fewshot_prompt(inst, demos)\n        elif args.prompt_mode == \"zeroshot\":\n            prompt = build_zeroshot_prompt(inst)\n        else:\n            prompt = None\n\n        record = {\n            \"id\": inst[\"id\"],\n            \"text\": inst[\"text\"],\n            \"entities\": inst.get(\"entities\", []),\n            \"relations\": inst.get(\"relations\", []),\n            \"events\": [],\n        }\n        if prompt is not None:\n            record[\"prompt\"] = prompt\n\n        output_instances.append(record)\n\n    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(args.output) or \".\", exist_ok=True)\n    with open(args.output, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        for inst in output_instances:\n            f.write(json.dumps(inst, ensure_ascii=False) + \"\\n\")\n\n    print(f\"Wrote {len(output_instances)} RE instances to {args.output}\")\n    print(f\"  Relation types in test set: {sorted({r['type'] for inst in output_instances for r in inst['relations']})}\")\n    print(f\"  Total gold relations: {sum(len(inst['relations']) for inst in output_instances)}\")\n\n    if args.demo_output and demos:\n        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(args.demo_output) or \".\", exist_ok=True)\n        with open(args.demo_output, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n            json.dump([dict(d) for d in demos], f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)\n        print(f\"Wrote {len(demos)} few-shot demos to {args.demo_output}\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n",
        "numLines": 179,
        "startLine": 1,
        "totalLines": 179
      }
    },
    "displayPath": "../../../artifacts/experiments/ace05_re/prepare_re_data.py"
  },

````json
{
  "parentUuid": "14c87299-a971-4fba-af10-8f14cebb23ed",
  "isSidechain": false,
  "attachment": {
    "type": "file",
    "filename": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_1/projects/struct_self_consist_ie/artifacts/experiments/ace05_re/spec.md",
    "content": {
      "type": "text",
      "file": {
        "filePath": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_1/projects/struct_self_consist_ie/artifacts/experiments/ace05_re/spec.md",
        "content": "# Experiment: exp_a3_ace05_re — Relation Extraction Self-Consistency Diagnostic\n\n## Objective\n\nValidate that the SC diagnostic cascade (degeneracy → agreement → LP-quality gap) generalizes from NER to Relation Extraction. RE is the closest structured prediction extension: same span-based output, but with entity pairs + relation types instead of single entity spans.\n\n## Data Source\n\n### Primary: SciERC (test split)\n\n- **Why SciERC, not ACE05**: ACE05 requires LDC license (restricted access, no public download). SciERC is publicly available, already integrated in the pipeline (`data_utils.load_scierc()`), and has both entity and relation annotations.\n- **Dataset**: SciERC test set (~551 sentences from scientific abstracts)\n- **Entity types** (6): Generic, Material, Method, Metric, OtherScientificTerm, Task\n- **Relation types** (7): COMPARE, CONJUNCTION, EVALUATE-FOR, FEATURE-OF, HYPONYM-OF, PART-OF, USED-FOR\n- **Format**: DyGIE++ JSON → converted to UIE format via `_parse_scierc_sentence()`\n- **Download**: `bash scripts/data/get_scierc.sh` from DyGIE++ repo, or already present on server\n\n### Filtering\n\nOnly instances with ≥1 gold relation are included in RE evaluation (skip relation-free sentences). Expected: ~200–300 instances after filtering.\n\n## Model\n\n- **Base**: Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct (same as NER experiments for controlled comparison)\n- **Prompt**: Few-shot RE prompt with schema hint\n- **Constrained decoding**: XGrammar with UIE_JSON_SCHEMA (already supports relations field)\n\n## Sampling Configuration\n\n| Parameter | Value |\n|-----------|-------|\n| N (samples) | 8 |\n| Temperature | 1.0 |\n| max_tokens | 1024 |\n| use_grammar | True |\n| collect_logprobs | True |\n| subtask | \"re\" |\n\nGreedy baseline: T=0, N=1 (automatic via `run_sampling_pipeline`).\n\n## Prompt Template\n\nUses `build_uie_prompt(text, subtask=\"re\", schema_hint=SCIERC_SCHEMA_HINT)` which produces:\n\n```\nExtract all relations (head entity, relation type, tail entity) from the following text.\nOutput a JSON object with the fields: entities, relations, events.\n\nText: {text}\nSchema: Entity types: Generic, Material, Method, Metric, OtherScientificTerm, Task\nRelation types: COMPARE, CONJUNCTION, EVALUATE-FOR, FEATURE-OF, HYPONYM-OF, PART-OF, USED-FOR\n\nOutput format: {\"entities\": [], \"relations\": [{\"head\": \"...\", \"tail\": \"...\", \"type\": \"...\", \"head_start\": &lt;int>, \"head_end\": &lt;int>, \"tail_start\": &lt;int>, \"tail_end\": &lt;int>}], \"events\": []}\n```\n\nAlternative: `use_train_format=True` for the train-aligned template (extracts entities + relations jointly).\n\n## Output JSON Schema\n\nSame as `UIE_JSON_SCHEMA` in sampling.py. Relation fields:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"head\": \"entity text\",\n  \"tail\": \"entity text\", \n  \"type\": \"USED-FOR\",\n  \"head_start\": 10,\n  \"head_end\": 25,\n  \"tail_start\": 30,\n  \"tail_end\": 45\n}\n```\n\n## Evaluation Metrics\n\n### Primary: Relation Strict Match F1\n\n`relation_strict_match()` in evaluation.py — (head_start, head_end, tail_start, tail_end, type) must all match exactly. Micro-averaged P/R/F1.\n\n### SC Diagnostic Metrics (per-instance)\n\n1. **Degeneracy**: All N samples produce identical relation sets → `is_degenerate_re()`\n2. **Fleiss' Kappa (surface)**: `fleiss_kappa_surface(samples, subtask=\"re\")` using (head, tail, type) surface keys\n3. **Structural Soft Jaccard**: `structural_consistency_soft_jaccard(samples, subtask=\"re\")` with `_re_soft_jaccard_pair()` — head/tail span overlap × type match via Hungarian matching\n4. **Oracle Best-of-N**: `oracle_best_of_n(samples, gold, subtask=\"re\")`\n5. **LP-weighted construction**: Adapted from NER consensus_construct to aggregate relation triples\n\n### SC Failure Mode Diagnostics (F1–F4)\n\n| Mode | Definition (RE adaptation) |\n|------|---------------------------|\n| F1: Degeneracy | All 8 samples produce identical relation sets |\n| F2: Low agreement | Fleiss' κ < 0.3 AND non-degenerate |\n| F3: High agreement, low quality | κ ≥ 0.3 but greedy F1 < 0.3 |\n| F4: LP-quality gap | construction_F1 − greedy_F1 < −0.05 (LP hurts) |\n\n## Pipeline Reuse\n\n| Component | Reuse | Notes |\n|-----------|-------|-------|\n| VLLMSampler | 100% | No changes needed |\n| XGrammar schema | 100% | UIE_JSON_SCHEMA already has relations |\n| build_uie_prompt | 100% | subtask=\"re\" template exists |\n| parse_extraction_output | 100% | _validate_relations() exists |\n| realign_spans | 100% | Handles relation head/tail spans |\n| run_sampling_pipeline | 100% | Pass subtask=\"re\" |\n| fleiss_kappa_surface | 100% | RE surface keys implemented |\n| structural_consistency_soft_jaccard | 100% | _re_soft_jaccard_pair() exists |\n| oracle_best_of_n | 100% | Uses per_instance_f1 with subtask=\"re\" |\n| relation_strict_match | 100% | Already in evaluation.py |\n| consensus_construct | ~30% | NER-specific, needs RE adaptation |\n| is_degenerate | ~30% | NER-specific, needs RE adaptation |\n| bootstrap test | ~50% | Needs RE-specific key extraction |\n\n## GPU Requirements\n\n- **Model**: Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct → ~15 GB VRAM\n- **Server**: autodl-5090 (single GPU sufficient)\n- **Estimated time**: ~15 min for 300 instances × 8 samples with vLLM batched inference\n- **Disk**: <100 MB for output JSONL\n\n## Dependencies\n\n```\nvllm, transformers, xgrammar, torch\nnumpy, scipy, datasets (for SciERC loading)\n```\n\nAll already installed on autodl-5090 from NER experiments.\n",
        "numLines": 130,
        "startLine": 1,
        "totalLines": 130
      }
    },
    "displayPath": "../../../artifacts/experiments/ace05_re/spec.md"
  },

}

  "text": "[severity: warning] SCD CoNLL 负控制实验设计存在混淆变量\n\n## 发现\n\nexp_b1_scd_conll_control 本应作为负控制（CoNLL 上 SC construction gain = -0.4pp，伪标签应无价值），但实际结果 post_scd_greedy_f1=0.9184 vs pre_scd_greedy_f1=0.9078，提升 +1.06pp。负控制未被确认。\n\n## 混淆变量分析\n\nSCD 训练数据 = 伪标签 1:1 混合金标数据。这意味着即使伪标签是垃圾，模型仍然获得了**额外的金标训练数据训练轮次**。+1.06pp 的提升可能完全来自：\n1. 额外的金标数据训练（与伪标签无关）\n2. 数据增广效应（即使噪声伪标签也有正则化作用）\n3. 真正的伪标签价值\n\n**无法区分这三个来源**。\n\n## 对论文 SCD 叙事的影响\n\n如果 FewNERD 上 SCD 提升（比如 +2pp）仅比 CoNLL 的 +1.06pp 略高，那么 SCD 的贡献可能主要来自额外训练而非伪标签质量。论文当前 framing 是\"SC 不仅是推理时策略，还能通过蒸馏改进基座模型\"——但如果改进源自额外金标训练而非伪标签，这个 claim 不成立。\n\n## 建议\n\n在 FewNERD SCD 结果出来后比较：\n- 若 FewNERD SCD delta >> CoNLL delta (+1.06pp) → 伪标签质量确实贡献了额外增益，SCD claim 有支撑\n- 若 FewNERD SCD delta ≈ CoNLL delta → 混淆未解，SCD claim 需降级\n- **理想控制组**：gold-only baseline（用相同量的额外金标数据训练，不混入伪标签），但 GPU-hours 可能不允许\n\n暂标 warning 而非 critical，因为 FewNERD 结果尚未出来，差异可能足够大以解决混淆。但 Agent 在写论文时必须诚实讨论此限制。",
  "project": "struct_self_consist_ie"
}

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Interesting data from the 14B N=1 inference on FewNERD:

- **greedy_f1 = 0.7985** — This is the 14B model's greedy performance on FewNERD. Compare with 7B: ~0.748 (Qwen3-8B-FT). So 14B is indeed better (+5pp).
- **oracle_f1 = 0.7541** — Wait, this is LOWER than greedy_f1? That's because oracle_f1 for N=1 is the same as sample F1 at T=1.0 (single sample). 

The oracle headroom is actually negative (-0.044) since the single sample at T=1.0 underperforms greedy. The mean sample F1 at 0.7349 is also lower than greedy as expected, and with N=1 there's no variation, so agreement metrics like Fleiss' kappa and soft Jaccard are trivially perfect while correlation metrics become undefined.

For the logprob baseline, I'm seeing moderate correlation with quality (rho=0.335, AUROC=0.691), which aligns with the framework. However, 11.3% of instances have zero F1 at T=1.0, which is concerning.

The 14B model achieves 0.7985 F1 on FewNERD—notably better than the 7B model's 0.748—so that's a useful scaling data point, though I'll need results at N=2, 4, and 8 to map out the full scaling curve. Looking at the instance counts, there are 37,648 total instances but 5,083 with empty gold labels and 2,615 with zero greedy F1, so there's a fair amount of filtering happening. Now I'm moving on to review the RE results.
```

1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
2	"""Relation Extraction Self-Consistency diagnostic experiment.
3	
4	Runs the full SC pipeline on SciERC RE data:
5	1. Load prepared RE instances (from prepare_re_data.py)
6	2. Sample N completions per instance via VLLMSampler + XGrammar
7	3. Compute RE F1 (greedy, majority vote, LP-weighted construction, oracle)
8	4. Compute SC diagnostic signals (degeneracy, Fleiss' κ, soft Jaccard)
9	5. Classify failure modes F1–F4
10	6. Run paired bootstrap test (construction vs greedy)
11	
12	Usage:
13	    # Full pipeline (sampling + evaluation)
14	    python run_re_sc.py \
15	        --model-path /root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct \
16	        --data /root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/data/re_test_instances.jsonl \
17	        --output-dir /root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/output/re_sc
18	
19	    # Evaluation only (skip sampling, load existing samples)
20	    python run_re_sc.py \
21	        --samples /path/to/re_samples.jsonl \
22	        --output-dir /path/to/output \
23	        --eval-only
24	"""
25	
26	import argparse
27	import json
28	import os
29	import statistics
30	import sys
31	from collections import Counter, defaultdict
32	
33	import numpy as np
34	
35	sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "../.."))
36	
37	from sampling import (
38	    VLLMSampler,
39	    SCIERC_SCHEMA_HINT,
40	    run_sampling_pipeline,
41	    load_sampled_results,
42	    save_sampled_results,
43	)
44	from evaluation import (
45	    per_instance_f1,
46	    relation_strict_match,
47	    compute_re_f1,
48	    _prf,
49	)
50	from consistency import (
51	    fleiss_kappa_surface,
52	    structural_consistency_soft_jaccard,
53	    oracle_best_of_n,
54	    compute_all_consistency_scores,
55	)
56	from data_utils import load_uie_jsonl
57	
58	
59	SUBTASK = "re"
60	
61	
62	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
63	# RE-specific consensus construction
64	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
65	
66	def relation_key(r: dict) -> tuple:
67	    return (r["head_start"], r["head_end"], r["tail_start"], r["tail_end"], r["type"])
68	
69	
70	def consensus_construct_re(
71	    inst: dict,
72	    high_thresh: float = 0.5,
73	    medium_thresh: float = 0.25,
74	) -> list[dict]:
75	    """LP-weighted relation construction (adapted from NER consensus_construct)."""
76	    samples = inst["samples"]
77	    N = len(samples)
78	
79	    rel_counts: Counter = Counter()
80	    rel_sample_indices: dict[tuple, list[int]] = defaultdict(list)
81	    rel_repr: dict[tuple, dict] = {}
82	
83	    for i, s in enumerate(samples):
84	        seen: set[tuple] = set()
85	        for r in s.get("relations", []):
86	            key = relation_key(r)
87	            if key not in seen:
88	                rel_counts[key] += 1
89	                rel_sample_indices[key].append(i)
90	                seen.add(key)
91	                if key not in rel_repr:
92	                    rel_repr[key] = r
93	
94	    sample_lps = []
95	    for i, s in enumerate(samples):
96	        lp = s.get("mean_logprob")
97	        if lp is None and "logprobs" in inst and i < len(inst["logprobs"]):
98	            lp = inst["logprobs"][i]
99	        if lp is not None:
100	            sample_lps.append(lp)
101	    instance_median_lp = statistics.median(sample_lps) if sample_lps else 0.0
102	
103	    consensus = []
104	    for key, count in rel_counts.items():
105	        freq = count / N
106	        if freq > high_thresh:
107	            consensus.append(rel_repr[key])
108	        elif freq > medium_thresh:
109	            contributing_lps = []
110	            for i in rel_sample_indices[key]:
111	                lp = samples[i].get("mean_logprob")
112	                if lp is None and "logprobs" in inst and i < len(inst["logprobs"]):
113	                    lp = inst["logprobs"][i]
114	                if lp is not None:
115	                    contributing_lps.append(lp)
116	            if contributing_lps:
117	                mean_lp = statistics.mean(contributing_lps)
118	                if mean_lp > instance_median_lp:
119	                    consensus.append(rel_repr[key])
120	
121	    return consensus
122	
123	
124	def majority_vote_re(
125	    samples: list[dict],
126	    threshold: float = 0.5,
127	) -> list[dict]:
128	    """Majority vote for relation triples at a given frequency threshold."""
129	    N = len(samples)
130	    rel_counts: Counter = Counter()
131	    rel_repr: dict[tuple, dict] = {}
132	
133	    for s in samples:
134	        seen: set[tuple] = set()
135	        for r in s.get("relations", []):
136	            key = relation_key(r)
137	            if key not in seen:
138	                rel_counts[key] += 1
139	                seen.add(key)
140	                if key not in rel_repr:
141	                    rel_repr[key] = r
142	
143	    return [rel_repr[key] for key, count in rel_counts.items() if count / N >= threshold]
144	
145	
146	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
147	# Degeneracy detection (RE-specific)
148	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
149	
150	def is_degenerate_re(inst: dict) -> bool:
151	    """All samples produce identical relation sets."""
152	    key_sets: set[frozenset] = set()
153	    for s in inst["samples"]:
154	        ks = frozenset(relation_key(r) for r in s.get("relations", []))
155	        key_sets.add(ks)
156	    return len(key_sets) == 1
157	
158	
159	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
160	# Failure mode classification
161	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
162	
163	def classify_failure_mode(
164	    inst: dict,
165	    greedy_f1: float,
166	    construction_f1: float,
167	    kappa: float,
168	) -> str | None:
169	    """Classify instance into F1–F4 failure modes.
170	
171	    Returns mode string or None if no failure detected.
172	    """
173	    degenerate = is_degenerate_re(inst)
174	
175	    if degenerate:
176	        return "F1_degeneracy"
177	    if kappa < 0.3:
178	        return "F2_low_agreement"
179	    if kappa >= 0.3 and greedy_f1 < 0.3:
180	        return "F3_high_agreement_low_quality"
181	    if construction_f1 - greedy_f1 < -0.05:
182	        return "F4_lp_quality_gap"
183	    return None
184	
185	
186	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
187	# Main evaluation logic
188	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
189	
190	def evaluate_re_sc(
191	    sampled_instances: list[dict],
192	    output_dir: str,
193	    high_thresh: float = 0.5,
194	    medium_thresh: float = 0.25,
195	) -> dict:
196	    """Run full RE SC diagnostic evaluation."""
197	    N_samples = len(sampled_instances[0]["samples"]) if sampled_instances else 0
198	    theta = 2 / N_samples if N_samples > 0 else 0.25
199	
200	    if medium_thresh is None:
201	        medium_thresh = theta
202	
203	    results = {
204	        "n_instances": len(sampled_instances),
205	        "n_samples": N_samples,
206	        "high_thresh": high_thresh,
207	        "medium_thresh": medium_thresh,
208	    }
209	
210	    # --- Per-instance metrics ---
211	    greedy_f1s = []
212	    construction_f1s = []
213	    majority_f1s = []
214	    oracle_f1s = []
215	    kappas = []
216	    soft_jaccards = []
217	    degen_flags = []
218	    failure_modes: list[str | None] = []
219	
220	    for inst in sampled_instances:
221	        gold = inst["gold"]
222	        samples = inst["samples"]
223	
224	        greedy = inst.get("greedy", samples[0])
225	        g_f1 = per_instance_f1(greedy, gold, subtask=SUBTASK)
226	        greedy_f1s.append(g_f1)
227	
228	        construction_rels = consensus_construct_re(inst, high_thresh, medium_thresh)
229	        c_pred = {"relations": construction_rels}
230	        c_f1 = per_instance_f1(c_pred, gold, subtask=SUBTASK)
231	        construction_f1s.append(c_f1)
232	
233	        mv_rels = majority_vote_re(samples, threshold=0.5)
234	        mv_pred = {"relations": mv_rels}
235	        mv_f1 = per_instance_f1(mv_pred, gold, subtask=SUBTASK)
236	        majority_f1s.append(mv_f1)
237	
238	        _, o_f1 = oracle_best_of_n(samples, gold, subtask=SUBTASK)
239	        oracle_f1s.append(o_f1)
240	
241	        kappa = fleiss_kappa_surface(samples, subtask=SUBTASK)
242	        kappas.append(kappa)
243	
244	        sj = structural_consistency_soft_jaccard(samples, subtask=SUBTASK)
245	        soft_jaccards.append(sj)
246	
247	        degen = is_degenerate_re(inst)
248	        degen_flags.append(degen)
249	
250	        fm = classify_failure_mode(inst, g_f1, c_f1, kappa)
251	        failure_modes.append(fm)
252	
253	    # --- Aggregate metrics ---
254	    results["greedy_f1_mean"] = float(np.mean(greedy_f1s))
255	    results["construction_f1_mean"] = float(np.mean(construction_f1s))
256	    results["majority_vote_f1_mean"] = float(np.mean(majority_f1s))
257	    results["oracle_f1_mean"] = float(np.mean(oracle_f1s))
258	    results["fleiss_kappa_mean"] = float(np.mean(kappas))
259	    results["soft_jaccard_mean"] = float(np.mean(soft_jaccards))
260	
261	    n = len(sampled_instances)
262	    n_degen = sum(degen_flags)
263	    results["degeneracy_rate"] = n_degen / n if n > 0 else 0.0
264	    results["n_degenerate"] = n_degen
265	    results["n_nondegenerate"] = n - n_degen
266	
267	    # --- Micro-averaged F1 ---
268	    greedy_preds = [inst.get("greedy", inst["samples"][0]) for inst in sampled_instances]
269	    golds = [inst["gold"] for inst in sampled_instances]
270	    results["greedy_micro_f1"] = compute_re_f1(greedy_preds, golds)
271	
272	    construction_preds = []
273	    for inst in sampled_instances:
274	        construction_preds.append({"relations": consensus_construct_re(inst, high_thresh, medium_thresh)})
275	    results["construction_micro_f1"] = compute_re_f1(construction_preds, golds)
276	
277	    # --- Failure mode distribution ---
278	    fm_counts: Counter = Counter()
279	    for fm in failure_modes:
280	        fm_counts[fm or "none"] += 1
281	    results["failure_modes"] = dict(fm_counts)
282	    results["failure_mode_pcts"] = {k: v / n * 100 for k, v in fm_counts.items()} if n > 0 else {}
283	
284	    # --- Non-degenerate stratification ---
285	    if n - n_degen > 0:
286	        nd_mask = [not d for d in degen_flags]
287	        nd_greedy = [f for f, m in zip(greedy_f1s, nd_mask) if m]
288	        nd_construction = [f for f, m in zip(construction_f1s, nd_mask) if m]
289	        results["nondegen_greedy_f1_mean"] = float(np.mean(nd_greedy))
290	        results["nondegen_construction_f1_mean"] = float(np.mean(nd_construction))
291	        results["nondegen_diff"] = results["nondegen_construction_f1_mean"] - results["nondegen_greedy_f1_mean"]
292	
293	    # --- Paired bootstrap test ---
294	    results["bootstrap"] = run_bootstrap_re(greedy_f1s, construction_f1s, degen_flags)
295	
296	    # --- Per-instance data (for downstream analysis) ---
297	    per_instance = []
298	    for i, inst in enumerate(sampled_instances):
299	        per_instance.append({
300	            "id": inst["id"],
301	            "greedy_f1": greedy_f1s[i],
302	            "construction_f1": construction_f1s[i],
303	            "majority_vote_f1": majority_f1s[i],
304	            "oracle_f1": oracle_f1s[i],
305	            "fleiss_kappa": kappas[i],
306	            "soft_jaccard": soft_jaccards[i],
307	            "degenerate": degen_flags[i],
308	            "failure_mode": failure_modes[i],
309	            "n_gold_relations": len(inst["gold"].get("relations", [])),
310	        })
311	
312	    # --- Save outputs ---
313	    os.makedirs(output_dir, exist_ok=True)
314	
315	    with open(os.path.join(output_dir, "re_sc_results.json"), "w") as f:
316	        json.dump(results, f, indent=2)
317	
318	    with open(os.path.join(output_dir, "re_per_instance.json"), "w") as f:
319	        json.dump(per_instance, f, indent=2)
320	
321	    print_summary(results)
322	    return results
323	
324	
325	def run_bootstrap_re(
326	    greedy_f1s: list[float],
327	    construction_f1s: list[float],
328	    degen_flags: list[bool],
329	    B: int = 10000,
330	    seed: int = 42,
331	) -> dict:
332	    """Paired bootstrap test: greedy vs LP-weighted construction for RE."""
333	    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
334	    n = len(greedy_f1s)
335	
336	    greedy_arr = np.array(greedy_f1s)
337	    construction_arr = np.array(construction_f1s)
338	    diffs = construction_arr - greedy_arr
339	
340	    obs_diff = float(diffs.mean())
341	
342	    boot_diffs = np.zeros(B)
343	    for b in range(B):
344	        idx = rng.randint(0, n, n)
345	        boot_diffs[b] = diffs[idx].mean()
346	    boot_diffs.sort()
347	
348	    ci_lo = float(boot_diffs[int(0.025 * B)])
349	    ci_hi = float(boot_diffs[int(0.975 * B)])
350	    p_value = float(np.mean(boot_diffs <= 0))
351	
352	    std_diff = float(diffs.std(ddof=1))
353	    cohens_d = obs_diff / std_diff if std_diff > 0 else float("inf")
354	
355	    result = {
356	        "observed_diff": obs_diff,
357	        "ci_95": [ci_lo, ci_hi],
358	        "p_value": p_value,
359	        "cohens_d": cohens_d,
360	        "B": B,
361	        "n_positive": int(np.sum(diffs > 0)),
362	        "n_negative": int(np.sum(diffs < 0)),
363	        "n_tied": int(np.sum(diffs == 0)),
364	    }
365	
366	    degen_arr = np.array(degen_flags, dtype=bool)
367	    nd_mask = ~degen_arr
368	    if nd_mask.sum() > 0:
369	        nd_diffs = diffs[nd_mask]
370	        nd_n = nd_diffs.shape[0]
371	        nd_boot = np.zeros(B)
372	        for b in range(B):
373	            idx = rng.randint(0, nd_n, nd_n)
374	            nd_boot[b] = nd_diffs[idx].mean()
375	        nd_boot.sort()
376	        result["nondegen_observed_diff"] = float(nd_diffs.mean())
377	        result["nondegen_ci_95"] = [float(nd_boot[int(0.025 * B)]), float(nd_boot[int(0.975 * B)])]
378	        result["nondegen_p_value"] = float(np.mean(nd_boot <= 0))
379	
380	    return result
381	
382	
383	def print_summary(results: dict):
384	    """Print human-readable summary."""
385	    n = results["n_instances"]
386	    print(f"\n{'='*60}")
387	    print(f"  RE Self-Consistency Diagnostic — {n} instances")
388	    print(f"{'='*60}")
389	    print(f"Greedy F1 (mean):         {results['greedy_f1_mean']:.4f}")
390	    print(f"Construction F1 (mean):   {results['construction_f1_mean']:.4f}")
391	    print(f"Majority Vote F1 (mean):  {results['majority_vote_f1_mean']:.4f}")
392	    print(f"Oracle F1 (mean):         {results['oracle_f1_mean']:.4f}")
393	    print(f"Fleiss' κ (mean):         {results['fleiss_kappa_mean']:.4f}")
394	    print(f"Soft Jaccard (mean):      {results['soft_jaccard_mean']:.4f}")
395	    print(f"Degeneracy rate:          {results['degeneracy_rate']:.2%} ({results['n_degenerate']}/{n})")
396	
397	    micro = results.get("greedy_micro_f1", {})
398	    print(f"\nMicro-avg greedy:         P={micro.get('precision',0):.4f} R={micro.get('recall',0):.4f} F1={micro.get('f1',0):.4f}")
399	    micro_c = results.get("construction_micro_f1", {})
400	    print(f"Micro-avg construction:   P={micro_c.get('precision',0):.4f} R={micro_c.get('recall',0):.4f} F1={micro_c.get('f1',0):.4f}")
401	
402	    if "nondegen_diff" in results:
403	        print(f"\nNon-degen greedy F1:      {results['nondegen_greedy_f1_mean']:.4f}")
404	        print(f"Non-degen construction:   {results['nondegen_construction_f1_mean']:.4f}")
405	        print(f"Non-degen diff:           {results['nondegen_diff']:+.4f}")
406	
407	    print(f"\nFailure mode distribution:")
408	    for mode, pct in sorted(results.get("failure_mode_pcts", {}).items()):
409	        print(f"  {mode}: {pct:.1f}%")
410	
411	    boot = results.get("bootstrap", {})
412	    if boot:
413	        print(f"\nBootstrap (B={boot.get('B', 10000)}):")
414	        print(f"  Diff: {boot.get('observed_diff', 0):+.4f}")
415	        ci = boot.get("ci_95", [0, 0])
416	        print(f"  95% CI: [{ci[0]:+.4f}, {ci[1]:+.4f}]")
417	        print(f"  p-value: {boot.get('p_value', 1):.4f}")
418	
419	
420	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
421	# Main
422	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
423	
424	def main():
425	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="RE Self-Consistency Diagnostic")
426	    parser.add_argument("--model-path", default=None,
427	                        help="Path to Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct model")
428	    parser.add_argument("--data", default=None,
429	                        help="Path to prepared RE instances JSONL (from prepare_re_data.py)")
430	    parser.add_argument("--samples", default=None,
431	                        help="Path to existing sampled results JSONL (skip sampling)")
432	    parser.add_argument("--output-dir", required=True,
433	                        help="Output directory")
434	    parser.add_argument("--eval-only", action="store_true",
435	                        help="Skip sampling, load --samples directly")
436	    parser.add_argument("--n-samples", type=int, default=8)
437	    parser.add_argument("--temperature", type=float, default=1.0)
438	    parser.add_argument("--max-tokens", type=int, default=1024)
439	    parser.add_argument("--tensor-parallel", type=int, default=1)
440	    parser.add_argument("--use-train-format", action="store_true",
441	                        help="Use train-aligned prompt template (joint NER+RE)")
442	    parser.add_argument("--high-thresh", type=float, default=0.5)
443	    parser.add_argument("--medium-thresh", type=float, default=None,
444	                        help="LP threshold (default: 2/N)")
445	    args = parser.parse_args()
446	
447	    if args.eval_only:
448	        if not args.samples:
449	            parser.error("--samples required when --eval-only is set")
450	        sampled = load_sampled_results(args.samples)
451	    else:
452	        if not args.model_path or not args.data:
453	            parser.error("--model-path and --data required for sampling")
454	
455	        instances = load_uie_jsonl(args.data)
456	        print(f"Loaded {len(instances)} RE instances")
457	
458	        sampler = VLLMSampler(
459	            model_path=args.model_path,
460	            tensor_parallel_size=args.tensor_parallel,
461	            max_model_len=4096,
462	            gpu_memory_utilization=0.90,
463	        )
464	
465	        sampled = run_sampling_pipeline(
466	            sampler=sampler,
467	            instances=instances,
468	            n_samples=args.n_samples,
469	            temperature=args.temperature,
470	            max_tokens=args.max_tokens,
471	            subtask="re" if not args.use_train_format else "full",
472	            schema_hint=SCIERC_SCHEMA_HINT,
473	            use_grammar=True,
474	            use_chat_template=True,
475	            use_train_format=args.use_train_format,
476	            output_path=os.path.join(args.output_dir, "re_samples.jsonl"),
477	            realign=True,
478	            collect_logprobs=True,
479	        )
480	        print(f"Sampled {len(sampled)} instances × {args.n_samples} samples")
481	
482	    evaluate_re_sc(
483	        sampled,
484	        output_dir=args.output_dir,
485	        high_thresh=args.high_thresh,
486	        medium_thresh=args.medium_thresh,
487	    )
488	
489	
490	if __name__ == "__main__":
491	    main()
492

  rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\n    n = len(greedy_f1s)\n\n    greedy_arr = np.array(greedy_f1s)\n    construction_arr = np.array(construction_f1s)\n    diffs = construction_arr - greedy_arr\n\n obs_diff / std_diff if std_diff > 0 else float(\"inf\")\n\n    result = {\n        \"observed_diff\": obs_diff,\n        \"ci_95\": [ci_lo, ci_hi],\n        \"p_value\": p_value,\n        \"cohens_d\": cohens_d,\n        \"B\": B,\n        \"n_positive\": int(np.sum(diffs > 0)),\n        \"n_negative\": int(np.sum(diffs < 0)),\n        \"n_tied\": int(np.sum(diffs == 0)),\n    }\n\n    degen_arr = np.array(degen_flags, dtype=bool)\n    nd_mask = ~degen_arr\n    if nd_mask.sum() > 0:\n        nd_diffs = diffs[nd_mask]\n        nd_n = nd_diffs.shape[0]\n        nd_boot = np.zeros(B)\n        for b in range(B):\n            idx = rng.randint(0, nd_n, nd_n)\n            nd_boot[b] = nd_diffs[idx].mean()\n        nd_boot.sort()\n        result[\"nondegen_observed_diff\"] = float(nd_diffs.mean())\n        result[\"nondegen_ci_95\"] = [float(nd_boot[int(0.025 * B)]), float(nd_boot[int(0.975 * B)])]\n        result[\"nondegen_p_value\"] = float(np.mean(nd_boot <= 0))\n\n    return result\n\n\ndef print_summary(results: dict):\n    \"\"\"Print human-readable summary.\"\"\"\n    n = results[\"n_instances\"]\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(f\"  RE Self-Consistency Diagnostic — {n} instances\")\n    print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n    print(f\"Greedy F1 (mean):         {results['greedy_f1_mean']:.4f}\")\n    print(f\"Construction F1 (mean):   {results['construction_f1_mean']:.4f}\")\n    print(f\"Majority Vote F1 (mean):  {results['majority_vote_f1_mean']:.4f}\")\n    print(f\"Oracle F1 (mean):         {results['oracle_f1_mean']:.4f}\")\n    print(f\"Fleiss' κ (mean):         {results['fleiss_kappa_mean']:.4f}\")\n    print(f\"Soft Jaccard (mean):      {results['soft_jaccard_mean']:.4f}\")\n    print(f\"Degeneracy rate:          {results['degeneracy_rate']:.2%} ({results['n_degenerate']}/{n})\")\n\n    micro = results.get(\"greedy_micro_f1\", {})\n    print(f\"\\nMicro-avg greedy:         P={micro.get('precision',0):.4f} R={micro.get('recall',0):.4f} F1={micro.get('f1',0):.4f}\")\n    micro_c = results.get(\"construction_micro_f1\", {})\n    print(f\"Micro-avg construction:   P={micro_c.get('precision',0):.4f} R={micro_c.get('recall',0):.4f} F1={micro_c.get('f1',0):.4f}\")\n\n    if \"nondegen_diff\" in results:\n        print(f\"\\nNon-degen greedy F1:      {results['nondegen_greedy_f1_mean']:.4f}\")\n        print(f\"Non-degen construction:   {results['nondegen_construction_f1_mean']:.4f}\")\n        print(f\"Non-degen diff:           {results['nondegen_diff']:+.4f}\")\n\n    print(f\"\\nFailure mode distribution:\")\n    for mode, pct in sorted(results.get(\"failure_mode_pcts\", {}).items()):\n        print(f\"  {mode}: {pct:.1f}%\")\n\n    boot = results.get(\"bootstrap\", {})\n    if boot:\n        print(f\"\\nBootstrap (B={boot.get('B', 10000)}):\")\n        print(f\"  Diff: {boot.get('observed_diff', 0):+.4f}\")\n        ci = boot.get(\"ci_95\", [0, 0])\n        print(f\"  95% CI: [{ci[0]:+.4f}, {ci[1]:+.4f}]\")\n        print(f\"  p-value: {boot.get('p_value', 1):.4f}\")\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Main\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"RE Self-Consistency Diagnostic\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--model-path\", default=None,\n                        help=\"Path to Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct model\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--data\", default=None,\n                        help=\"Path to prepared RE instances JSONL (from prepare_re_data.py)\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--samples\", default=None,\n                        help=\"Path to existing sampled results JSONL (skip sampling)\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--output-dir\", required=True,\n                        help=\"Output directory\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--eval-only\", action=\"store_true\",\n                        help=\"Skip sampling, load --samples directly\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--n-samples\", type=int, default=8)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--temperature\", type=float, default=1.0)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--max-tokens\", type=int, default=1024)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--tensor-parallel\", type=int, default=1)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--use-train-format\", action=\"store_true\",\n                        help=\"Use train-aligned prompt template (joint NER+RE)\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--high-thresh\", type=float, default=0.5)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--medium-thresh\", type=float, default=None,\n                        help=\"LP threshold (default: 2/N)\")\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    if args.eval_only:\n        if not args.samples:\n            parser.error(\"--samples required when --eval-only is set\")\n        sampled = load_sampled_results(args.samples)\n    else:\n        if not args.model_path or not args.data:\n            parser.error(\"--model-path and --data required for sampling\")\n\n        instances = load_uie_jsonl(args.data)\n        print(f\"Loaded {len(instances)} RE instances\")\n\n        sampler = VLLMSampler(\n            model_path=args.model_path,\n            tensor_parallel_size=args.tensor_parallel,\n            max_model_len=4096,\n            gpu_memory_utilization=0.90,\n        )\n\n        sampled = run_sampling_pipeline(\n            sampler=sampler,\n            instances=instances,\n            n_samples=args.n_samples,\n The Spider SQL SC experiment worker has completed code preparation. Let me review what was delivered:

3. **Model**: Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct (base instruct, not fine-tuned)
4. **GPU**: cuda:2 on autodl-5090-b
5. **Data**: Spider validation 1034 → random 200 sample
6. **Key issue**: Spider original databases unavailable (Google Drive blocked by AutoDL proxy), so synthetic databases are used


7. **Registry**: `exp_spider_sc_pilot` — PENDING (not in QUEUED list, might be in PENDING)

The synthetic databases show 78% non-empty results on gold queries, meaning 22% return empty results. This could impact execution accuracy, but the SC diagnostic metrics should remain valid since they compare model outputs against each other rather than against gold standards. The base Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct model isn't fine-tuned on SQL, which is a limitation for cross-task generalization.

GPU 2 on autodl-5090-b is available—GPU 0 and 3 are running SCD eval phases while GPU 1 is idle. The 200-sample pilot size is reasonable for testing whether SC diagnostics generalize to SQL, though the 78% execution rate does raise concerns about the reliability of execution accuracy metrics.

Now I need to track down the actual status of `exp_spider_sc_pilot` in the registry—the worker mentioned "PENDING" rather than "QUEUED", so I should check what status it actually has and then review the script itself to assess its quality.

````text
1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
2	"""Spider SQL Self-Consistency Pilot Experiment.
3	
4	Validates SC diagnostic cascade (degeneracy, LP-quality gap,
5	agreement-correctness correlation) on SQL structured generation.
6	- Model: Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct (base, not NER-finetuned)
7	- Data: Spider dev set, 200 random examples (seed=42)
8	- Sampling: N=8, T=1.0
9	- Evaluation: execution accuracy (execute SQL on SQLite databases)
10	- Selection: greedy vs MV (on execution results) vs LP vs oracle
11	"""
12	
13	import json
14	import os
15	import random
16	import signal
17	import sqlite3
18	import time
19	from collections import Counter
20	from pathlib import Path
21	
22	import numpy as np
23	from datasets import load_dataset
24	from scipy.stats import spearmanr, kendalltau
25	
26	SEED = 42
27	N_SAMPLES = 8
28	TEMPERATURE = 1.0
29	N_EXAMPLES = 200
30	N_FEWSHOT = 3
31	MODEL_ID = "Qwen/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct"
32	SPIDER_DB_DIR = "/root/autodl-tmp/spider_data/database"
33	OUTPUT_DIR = "/root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/output/spider_sc_pilot"
34	GPU_ID = 2
35	SQL_TIMEOUT = 5
36	MAX_MODEL_LEN = 8192
37	
38	
39	# ── Utilities ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
40	
41	class SQLTimeoutError(Exception):
42	    pass
43	
44	
45	def _timeout_handler(signum, frame):
46	    raise SQLTimeoutError("SQL execution timed out")
47	
48	
49	def set_seed(seed):
50	    random.seed(seed)
51	    np.random.seed(seed)
52	
53	
54	# ── Data Loading ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
55	
56	def load_spider_data():
57	    ds = load_dataset("xlangai/spider", split="validation")
58	    train_ds = load_dataset("xlangai/spider", split="train")
59	    indices = list(range(len(ds)))
60	    random.shuffle(indices)
61	    sampled = [ds[i] for i in indices[:N_EXAMPLES]]
62	    return sampled, train_ds
63	
64	
65	# ── Schema Extraction ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
66	
67	_schema_cache: dict[str, str | None] = {}
68	
69	
70	def get_schema(db_id: str) -> str | None:
71	    if db_id in _schema_cache:
72	        return _schema_cache[db_id]
73	
74	    db_path = os.path.join(SPIDER_DB_DIR, db_id, f"{db_id}.sqlite")
75	    if not os.path.exists(db_path):
76	        _schema_cache[db_id] = None
77	        return None
78	
79	    conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)
80	    cur = conn.cursor()
81	    cur.execute(
82	        "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name NOT LIKE 'sqlite_%'"
83	    )
84	    tables = [r[0] for r in cur.fetchall()]
85	
86	    parts = []
87	    for tbl in tables:
88	        cur.execute(f'PRAGMA table_info("{tbl}")')
89	        columns = cur.fetchall()
90	        col_strs = []
91	        pk_cols = []
92	        for _, name, ctype, notnull, _, pk in columns:
93	            s = f"  {name} {ctype or 'TEXT'}"
94	            if notnull:
95	                s += " NOT NULL"
96	            col_strs.append(s)
97	            if pk:
98	                pk_cols.append(name)
99	        if pk_cols:
100	            col_strs.append(f"  PRIMARY KEY ({', '.join(pk_cols)})")
101	
102	        cur.execute(f'PRAGMA foreign_key_list("{tbl}")')
103	        for fk in cur.fetchall():
104	            _, _, ref_tbl, from_col, to_col, *_ = fk
105	            col_strs.append(f"  FOREIGN KEY ({from_col}) REFERENCES {ref_tbl}({to_col})")
106	
107	        parts.append(f"CREATE TABLE {tbl} (\n" + ",\n".join(col_strs) + "\n);")
108	
109	    conn.close()
110	    schema = "\n\n".join(parts)
111	    _schema_cache[db_id] = schema
112	    return schema
113	
114	
115	# ── Prompt Construction ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
116	
117	_train_by_db: dict[str, list] = {}
118	
119	
120	def _index_train_by_db(train_ds):
121	    global _train_by_db
122	    if _train_by_db:
123	        return
124	    for i in range(len(train_ds)):
125	        db = train_ds[i]["db_id"]
126	        _train_by_db.setdefault(db, []).append(i)
127	
128	
129	def get_fewshot(train_ds, db_id: str, n: int = N_FEWSHOT) -> list:
130	    _index_train_by_db(train_ds)
131	    same = _train_by_db.get(db_id, [])
132	    if len(same) >= n:
133	        chosen = random.sample(same, n)
134	    else:
135	        others = [
136	            i for db, idxs in _train_by_db.items() if db != db_id for i in idxs
137	        ]
138	        chosen = same + random.sample(others, min(n - len(same), len(others)))
139	    return [train_ds[i] for i in chosen[:n]]
140	
141	
142	def build_prompt(example: dict, train_ds) -> str | None:
143	    schema = get_schema(example["db_id"])
144	    if schema is None:
145	        return None
146	
147	    fs_examples = get_fewshot(train_ds, example["db_id"])
148	    blocks = []
149	    for fs in fs_examples:
150	        fs_schema = get_schema(fs["db_id"])
151	        if fs_schema is None:
152	            continue
153	        blocks.append(
154	            f"Database schema:\n{fs_schema}\n\nQuestion: {fs['question']}\nSQL: {fs['query']}"
155	        )
156	
157	    blocks.append(
158	        f"Database schema:\n{schema}\n\nQuestion: {example['question']}\nSQL:"
159	    )
160	    return "\n\n---\n\n".join(blocks)
161	
162	
163	# ── SQL Execution ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
164	
165	
166	def execute_sql(db_id: str, sql: str):
167	    """Execute SQL, return (result_rows, error_string)."""
168	    db_path = os.path.join(SPIDER_DB_DIR, db_id, f"{db_id}.sqlite")
169	    if not os.path.exists(db_path):
170	        return None, f"db not found: {db_path}"
171	
172	    old = signal.signal(signal.SIGALRM, _timeout_handler)
173	    signal.alarm(SQL_TIMEOUT)
174	    try:
175	        conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)
176	        conn.execute("PRAGMA query_only = ON")
177	        cur = conn.cursor()
178	        cur.execute(sql)
179	        rows = cur.fetchall()
180	        conn.close()
181	        signal.alarm(0)
182	        return rows, None
183	    except SQLTimeoutError:
184	        signal.alarm(0)
185	        return None, "timeout"
186	    except Exception as e:
187	        signal.alarm(0)
188	        return None, str(e)
189	    finally:
190	        signal.signal(signal.SIGALRM, old)
191	
192	
193	def normalize_result(rows):
194	    if rows is None:
195	        return None
196	    try:
197	        return tuple(sorted(tuple(str(v) for v in r) for r in rows))
198	    except TypeError:
199	        return tuple(tuple(str(v) for v in r) for r in rows)
200	
201	
202	def extract_sql(text: str) -> str:
203	    text = text.strip()
204	    for prefix in ("```sql", "```SQL", "```"):
205	        if text.startswith(prefix):
206	            text = text[len(prefix) :]
207	    if text.endswith("```"):
208	        text = text[:-3]
209	    text = text.strip()
210	
211	    lines = [l.strip() for l in text.split("\n") if l.strip() and not l.strip().startswith("--")]
212	    sql = " ".join(lines)
213	    while sql.endswith(";"):
214	        sql = sql[:-1].strip()
215	    return sql
216	
217	
218	# ── Main ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
219	
220	
221	def main():
222	    set_seed(SEED)
223	    os.makedirs(OUTPUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)
224	    os.environ["CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES"] = str(GPU_ID)
225	
226	    if not os.path.isdir(SPIDER_DB_DIR):
227	        print(f"ERROR: Spider databases not found at {SPIDER_DB_DIR}")
228	        print("Run: git clone https://github.com/taoyds/spider.git /root/autodl-tmp/spider_data")
229	        return
230	
231	    # Load data
232	    print("Loading Spider data...")
233	    examples, train_ds = load_spider_data()
234	    print(f"Sampled {len(examples)} examples from Spider dev ({len(set(e['db_id'] for e in examples))} databases)")
235	
236	    # Build prompts
237	    print("Building prompts...")
238	    valid_examples = []
239	    prompts = []
240	    for ex in examples:
241	        p = build_prompt(ex, train_ds)
242	        if p is not None:
243	            valid_examples.append(ex)
244	            prompts.append(p)
245	    print(f"Valid: {len(valid_examples)}/{len(examples)}")
246	
247	    SYSTEM_MSG = (
248	        "You are a SQL expert. Given a database schema and a question, "
249	        "write the SQL query that answers the question. "
250	        "Output only the SQL query, nothing else."
251	    )
252	    chat_prompts = [
253	        [{"role": "system", "content": SYSTEM_MSG}, {"role": "user", "content": p}]
254	        for p in prompts
255	    ]
256	
257	    # vLLM
258	    from vllm import LLM, SamplingParams
259	
260	    print(f"Loading {MODEL_ID} on cuda:{GPU_ID}...")
261	    llm = LLM(
262	        model=MODEL_ID,
263	        tensor_parallel_size=1,
264	        max_model_len=MAX_MODEL_LEN,
265	        gpu_memory_utilization=0.90,
266	        trust_remote_code=True,
267	    )
268	
269	    # N=8 stochastic sampling
270	    print(f"Sampling N={N_SAMPLES}, T={TEMPERATURE}...")
271	    t0 = time.time()
272	    sp = SamplingParams(
273	        n=N_SAMPLES,
274	        temperature=TEMPERATURE,
275	        max_tokens=256,
276	        logprobs=1,
277	        stop=["\n\n"],
278	    )
279	    sample_out = llm.chat(chat_prompts, sp)
280	    t_sample = time.time() - t0
281	    print(f"Sampling done: {t_sample:.1f}s")
282	
283	    # Greedy
284	    print("Greedy decoding...")
285	    t0 = time.time()
286	    gp = SamplingParams(
287	        n=1,
288	        temperature=0.0,
289	        max_tokens=256,
290	        logprobs=1,
291	        stop=["\n\n"],
292	    )
293	    greedy_out = llm.chat(chat_prompts, gp)
294	    t_greedy = time.time() - t0
295	    print(f"Greedy done: {t_greedy:.1f}s")
296	
297	    # ── Evaluate ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
298	    print("Evaluating...")
299	    results = []
300	    agg = dict(
301	        greedy=0, mv=0, lp=0, oracle=0,
302	        n_degen=0, n_exec_err=0,
303	    )
304	    agreement_list = []
305	    correctness_list = []
306	
307	    for i, ex in enumerate(valid_examples):
308	        db_id = ex["db_id"]
309	        gold_q = ex["query"]
310	
311	        gold_rows, gold_err = execute_sql(db_id, gold_q)
312	        if gold_err:
313	            results.append(dict(
314	                idx=i, db_id=db_id, question=ex["question"],
315	                gold_query=gold_q, gold_error=gold_err, skipped=True,
316	            ))
317	            continue
318	
319	        gold_norm = normalize_result(gold_rows)
320	
321	        # Samples
322	        sqls, norms, corrects, mean_lps = [], [], [], []
323	        for out in sample_out[i].outputs:
324	            sql = extract_sql(out.text)
325	            sqls.append(sql)
326	            rows, err = execute_sql(db_id, sql)
327	            n = normalize_result(rows)
328	            ok = (n == gold_norm) and (err is None)
329	            norms.append(n)
330	            corrects.append(ok)
331	            n_tok = len(out.token_ids)
332	            clp = out.cumulative_logprob if out.cumulative_logprob is not None else 0.0
333	            mean_lps.append(clp / max(n_tok, 1))
334	
335	        # Greedy
336	        g_sql = extract_sql(greedy_out[i].outputs[0].text)
337	        g_rows, g_err = execute_sql(db_id, g_sql)
338	        g_norm = normalize_result(g_rows)
339	        g_ok = (g_norm == gold_norm) and (g_err is None)
340	
341	        # MV on execution results
342	        rc = Counter(r for r in norms if r is not None)
343	        mv_ok = (rc.most_common(1)[0][0] == gold_norm) if rc else False
344	
345	        # LP selection (mean logprob)
346	        lp_idx = int(np.argmax(mean_lps))
347	        lp_ok = corrects[lp_idx]
348	
349	        # Oracle
350	        oracle_ok = any(corrects)
351	
352	        # Degeneracy
353	        uniq = set(sqls)
354	        degen = len(uniq) == 1
355	
356	        # Agreement / correctness
357	        agree = rc.most_common(1)[0][1] / N_SAMPLES if rc else 0.0
358	        cfrac = sum(corrects) / N_SAMPLES
359	
360	        agg["greedy"] += g_ok
361	        agg["mv"] += mv_ok
362	        agg["lp"] += lp_ok
363	        agg["oracle"] += oracle_ok
364	        agg["n_degen"] += degen
365	        agg["n_exec_err"] += sum(1 for r in norms if r is None)
366	        agreement_list.append(agree)
367	        correctness_list.append(cfrac)
368	
369	        results.append(dict(
370	            idx=i, db_id=db_id, question=ex["question"],
371	            gold_query=gold_q, greedy_sql=g_sql, greedy_correct=g_ok,
372	            sample_sqls=sqls, sample_correct=corrects, sample_mean_lp=mean_lps,
373	            n_unique_sqls=len(uniq),
374	            n_unique_results=len(set(r for r in norms if r is not None)),
375	            is_degenerate=degen, mv_correct=mv_ok,
376	            lp_correct=lp_ok, oracle_correct=oracle_ok,
377	            agreement=agree, correct_frac=cfrac, skipped=False,
378	        ))
379	
380	    # ── Summary ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
381	    valid = [r for r in results if not r.get("skipped")]
382	    nv = len(valid)
383	    if nv == 0:
384	        print("ERROR: no valid results")
385	        return
386	
387	    g_acc = agg["greedy"] / nv
388	    mv_acc = agg["mv"] / nv
389	    lp_acc = agg["lp"] / nv
390	    o_acc = agg["oracle"] / nv
391	    degen_rate = agg["n_degen"] / nv
392	
393	    sp_rho, sp_p, kt_tau, kt_p = np.nan, np.nan, np.nan, np.nan
394	    if len(agreement_list) > 2:
395	        sp_rho, sp_p = spearmanr(agreement_list, correctness_list)
396	        kt_tau, kt_p = kendalltau(agreement_list, correctness_list)
397	
398	    f1 = agg["n_degen"]
399	    f2 = sum(1 for r in valid if not r["lp_correct"] and not r["is_degenerate"])
400	    f3 = sum(1 for r in valid if r["mv_correct"] != r["oracle_correct"])
401	
402	    summary = dict(
403	        config=dict(
404	            model=MODEL_ID, n_samples=N_SAMPLES, temperature=TEMPERATURE,
405	            n_examples=N_EXAMPLES, n_fewshot=N_FEWSHOT, seed=SEED,
406	            task="text-to-sql", dataset="spider-dev",
407	        ),
408	        counts=dict(
409	            total=len(examples), valid=nv,
410	            skipped_gold_error=len(results) - nv,
411	        ),
412	        accuracy=dict(
413	            greedy=round(g_acc, 4), majority_voting=round(mv_acc, 4),
414	            lp_selection=round(lp_acc, 4), oracle=round(o_acc, 4),
415	        ),
416	        sc_gain=dict(
417	            mv_minus_greedy=round(mv_acc - g_acc, 4),
418	            oracle_minus_greedy=round(o_acc - g_acc, 4),
419	        ),
420	        diagnostics=dict(
421	            degeneracy_rate=round(degen_rate, 4),
422	            lp_quality_gap=round(1 - lp_acc, 4),
423	            spearman_rho=round(float(sp_rho), 4) if not np.isnan(sp_rho) else None,
424	            spearman_p=float(f"{sp_p:.6f}") if not np.isnan(sp_p) else None,
425	            kendall_tau=round(float(kt_tau), 4) if not np.isnan(kt_tau) else None,
426	            kendall_p=float(f"{kt_p:.6f}") if not np.isnan(kt_p) else None,
427	        ),
428	        failure_modes=dict(
429	            F1_degeneracy=f1, F1_rate=round(f1 / nv, 4),
430	            F2_lp_gap=f2, F2_rate=round(f2 / nv, 4),
431	            F3_agree_ne_correct=f3, F3_rate=round(f3 / nv, 4),
432	        ),
433	        exec_errors_total=agg["n_exec_err"],
434	        timing=dict(sampling_sec=round(t_sample, 1), greedy_sec=round(t_greedy, 1)),
435	    )
436	
437	    with open(os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, "per_instance.json"), "w") as f:
438	        json.dump(results, f, indent=2, default=str)
439	    with open(os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, "summary.json"), "w") as f:
440	        json.dump(summary, f, indent=2)
441	
442	    print(f"\n{'='*60}")
443	    print("SPIDER SQL SC PILOT RESULTS")
444	    print(f"{'='*60}")
445	    print(f"Valid: {nv}/{len(examples)}")
446	    print(f"\nExecution Accuracy:")
447	    print(f"  Greedy:    {g_acc:.4f}")
448	    print(f"  MV:        {mv_acc:.4f}  ({mv_acc - g_acc:+.4f} vs greedy)")
449	    print(f"  LP:        {lp_acc:.4f}  ({lp_acc - g_acc:+.4f} vs greedy)")
450	    print(f"  Oracle:    {o_acc:.4f}  ({o_acc - g_acc:+.4f} vs greedy)")
451	    print(f"\nDiagnostics:")

453	    print(f"  LP-quality gap: {1-lp_acc:.4f}")

456	    print(f"\nFailure Modes:")

SQL on SQLite databases)\n- Selection: greedy vs MV (on execution results) vs LP vs oracle\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport os\nimport random\nimport signal\nimport sqlite3\nimport time\nfrom [ds[i] for i in indices[:N_EXAMPLES]]\n    return sampled, train_ds\n\n\n# ── Schema Extraction ───────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n_schema_cache: dict[str, str | None] = {}\n\n\ndef get_schema(db_id: str) -> str | None:\n    if db_id in _schema_cache:\n        return _schema_cache[db_id]\n\n    db_path = os.path.join(SPIDER_DB_DIR, db_id, f\"{db_id}.sqlite\")\n    if not os.path.exists(db_path):\n        _schema_cache[db_id] = None\n        return None\n\n    conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)\n    cur = conn.cursor()\n    cur.execute(\n        \"SELECT name FROM table_info(\"{tbl}\")')\n        columns = cur.fetchall()\n        col_strs = []\n        pk_cols = []\n        for _, name, ctype, notnull, _, pk in columns:\n            s = f\"  {name} {ctype or 'TEXT'}\"\n            if notnull:\n                s += \" NOT NULL\"\n            col_strs.append(s)\n            if pk:\n                pk_cols.append(name)\n        if pk_cols:\n            col_strs.append(f\"  PRIMARY KEY ({', '.join(pk_cols)})\")\n\n        cur.execute(f'PRAGMA foreign_key_list(\"{tbl}\")')\n        for fk in cur.fetchall():\n            _, _, ref_tbl, from_col, to_col, *_ = fk\n            col_strs.append(f\"  FOREIGN KEY ({from_col}) REFERENCES {ref_tbl}({to_col})\")\n\n        parts.append(f\"CREATE TABLE {tbl} (\\n\" + \",\\n\".join(col_strs) + \"\\n);\")\n\n    conn.close()\n    schema = \"\\n\\n\".join(parts)\n    _schema_cache[db_id] = schema\n    return schema\n\n\n# ── Prompt Construction ─────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n_train_by_db: dict[str, list] = {}\n\n\ndef _index_train_by_db(train_ds):\n    global _train_by_db\n    if _train_by_db:\n        return\n    for i in range(len(train_ds)):\n        db = train_ds[i][\"db_id\"]\n        _train_by_db.setdefault(db, []).append(i)\n\n\ndef get_fewshot(train_ds, db_id: str, n: int = N_FEWSHOT) -> list:\n    _index_train_by_db(train_ds)\n    same = _train_by_db.get(db_id, [])\n    if len(same) >= n:\n        chosen = random.sample(same, n)\n    else:\n        others = [\n            i for db, idxs in _train_by_db.items() if db != db_id for i in idxs\n        ]\n        chosen = same + random.sample(others, min(n - len(same), len(others)))\n    return [train_ds[i] for i in chosen[:n]]\n\n\ndef build_prompt(example: dict, train_ds) -> str | None:\n    schema = get_schema(example[\"db_id\"])\n    if schema is None:\n        return None\n\n    fs_examples = get_fewshot(train_ds, example[\"db_id\"])\n    blocks = []\n    for fs in fs_examples:\n        fs_schema = get_schema(fs[\"db_id\"])\n        if fs_schema is None:\n            continue\n        blocks.append(\n {example['question']}\\nSQL:\"\n    )\n    return \"\\n\\n---\\n\\n\".join(blocks)\n\n\n# ── SQL Execution ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n\ndef execute_sql(db_id: str, sql: None, f\"db not found: {db_path}\"\n\n    old = signal.signal(signal.SIGALRM, _timeout_handler)\n    signal.alarm(SQL_TIMEOUT)\n    try:\n        conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)\n        conn.execute(\"PRAGMA query_only = ON\")\n        cur = conn.cursor()\n        cur.execute(sql)\n        rows = cur.fetchall()\n        conn.close()\n        signal.alarm(0)\n        return rows,
