<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Let me now review the Spider SQL SC pilot code in detail:

**Code Review of `run_spider_sc.py`:**

1. **Constants (lines 27-35):**
   - SEED=42, N_SAMPLES=8, T=1.0, N_EXAMPLES=200, N_FEWSHOT=3 ✓
   - MODEL_ID="Qwen/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct" — base model, correct for cross-task pilot ✓
   - GPU_ID=2 — matches worker report ✓
   - SQL_TIMEOUT=5s — reasonable ✓

2. **Data Loading (lines 56-62):**
   - Uses `xlangai/spider` from HuggingFace ✓
   - Random 200 from validation ✓
   - **Issue**: `random.shuffle(indices)` uses the global seed set in `set_seed()` — reproducible ✓

3. **Schema Extraction (lines 67-112):**
   - Reads SQLite databases to extract CREATE TABLE DDL ✓
   - Includes foreign keys ✓
   - Uses schema cache ✓

4. **Prompt Construction (lines 117-160):**
   - Building the few-shot examples with schema context and expected SQL outputs...

5. **SQL Execution (lines 166-215):**
   - Setting up timeout handling with SIGALRM for Linux environments, enforcing read-only mode with PRAGMA, and normalizing results by sorting rows and converting to strings for accurate comparison ✓
   - Extracting SQL from markdown code blocks and stripping comments ✓

6. **Main Evaluation (lines 221-461):**
   - Running greedy decoding separately from stochastic sampling, then evaluating with multiple metrics: greedy execution accuracy, majority voting on actual execution results rather than SQL text (which is more robust), LP selection based on highest mean logprob, and oracle selection for any correct sample ✓
   - Measuring degeneracy as complete SQL text uniformity across all 8 outputs, agreement as the fraction of samples matching the majority result, and correlation between agreement and correctness using Spearman and Kendall tests ✓

7. Defining failure modes...

Wait, I'm noticing a discrepancy between how the NER task defines failure modes and what's actually in the SQL code. The NER approach breaks it down into degeneracy, low agreement, high agreement with low quality, and LP-quality gaps, but the SQL implementation seems to be tracking something different. Let me trace through what's actually being measured here.

The SQL definitions are checking for LP correctness and whether the oracle and majority vote agree, which doesn't directly map to the NER framework. This is a significant mismatch—the failure mode labels don't align with the paper's conceptual categories. Though I understand this is a cross-task pilot and SQL evaluation works differently since it's binary rather than F1-based, the inconsistency should at least be documented or the labels should be adjusted to match the actual logic being implemented.

The LP-quality gap is another example of this drift: it's defined as LP's error rate rather than the construction-versus-greedy F1 difference from the NER paper. These are fundamentally different metrics sharing the same name, which could cause confusion.

Some metrics from the NER setup don't translate directly to SQL—Fleiss' κ and structural consistency don't apply when there are no spans to compare, so using agreement on execution results is a reasonable adaptation. Similarly, LP-weighted construction doesn't make sense for SQL since you can't merge queries, so selection by log probability is the right approach. A bootstrap test could be useful but isn't essential for a pilot study.

On security, the code prevents SQL injection through write operations and stops infinite loops, but there's still exposure to SQLite extensions like `readfile()` that could access files—though for a controlled experiment with synthetic data, this is acceptable.

The main issues I'm seeing: the synthetic databases have a 22% failure rate on gold queries, which gets skipped and reduces the effective sample size to around 156 from 200, weakening statistical power. The failure mode definitions diverge from the NER framework's F1-F4 structure, making cross-task comparison harder. And the agreement metric is purely frequency-based rather than using something like Fleiss' κ, which accounts for chance agreement.

There's also a semantic degeneracy problem—comparing SQL strings with `len(set(sqls)) == 1` misses semantically equivalent queries with different formatting. Checking execution results instead would be more accurate. The experiment registration is missing from the registry, and I need to verify the generate_spider_dbs.py script exists and check if everything's properly set up.
```

<details open><summary>🔧 Tool call — mcp__agent-ml-research__reviewer_post_to_director · `toolu_013R6sdCAbhjyD69uZVrQ67P`</summary>

```json
{
  "text": "[severity: warning] Spider SQL SC Pilot (exp_spider_sc_pilot) 代码审查——3 个设计问题需关注\n\n## 审查对象\nWorker 交付的 `run_spider_sc.py` (466行)，用于验证 SC 诊断框架在 text-to-SQL 任务上的跨任务泛化性。\n\n## 发现\n\n### 1. [severity: warning] Failure mode 定义与 NER 框架不一致\n\nNER 框架的 F1-F4 定义（degeneracy / low agreement / high-agree-low-quality / LP-quality gap）在 SQL 代码中被替换为完全不同的语义：\n\n| Mode | NER 定义 | SQL 代码实际定义 |\n|------|----------|-----------------|\n| F2 | κ < 0.3 且非退化 | LP 选择失败且非退化 (line 399) |\n| F3 | κ ≥ 0.3 且 greedy F1 < 0.3 | MV ≠ oracle (line 400) |\n\n`lp_quality_gap` 也被重新定义为 `1 - lp_acc`（LP 选择错误率），而非 NER 框架的 `construction_F1 - greedy_F1`。\n\n**影响**: 论文若声称\"SC 诊断框架跨任务泛化\"，failure mode 定义必须在概念上对齐。当前定义无法支撑跨任务的 F-mode 分布对比。\n\n### 2. [severity: warning] 退化检测基于文本而非语义\n\n`is_degenerate` 定义为 `len(set(sqls)) == 1`（8 个 SQL 字符串完全相同）。但两个语义等价但格式不同的 SQL（如 `SELECT a FROM t` vs `select a from t`）不会被判定为退化。这会系统性低估退化率。\n\n建议改用执行结果比较：`len(set(normalize_result(execute(sql)) for sql in sqls)) == 1`。\n\n### 3. [severity: info] 合成数据库的 22% gold 失败率\n\nsynthetic databases 使得 22% 的 gold queries 失败（被 skip），有效样本从 200 降至 ~156。对 SC 诊断指标（内部一致性）影响有限，但绝对 execution accuracy 不可与原版 Spider 对比。Worker 已正确说明此限制。\n\n### 4. [severity: info] Registry 缺失\n\n`exp_spider_sc_pilot` 在 registry 中不存在。Worker 报告 status=\"PENDING\" 但实际未注册。需补注册。\n\n## GPU 边界\ncuda:2 on autodl-5090-b — 在白名单内 ✓\n\n## 建议\n问题 1 和 2 在实际运行前修复成本低。特别是 failure mode 定义对齐——这是跨任务泛化叙事的核心支撑点，定义不一致会被审稿人直接攻击。",
  "project": "struct_self_consist_ie"
}
```

```text
1	{
2	  "raw": "Registry 已更新完毕。\n\n**总结**：`exp_backup3_distill_verifier` 实验完成，conclusion = **negative**。\n\n- Entity-level AUROC 不错（~0.875），说明分类器能区分 gold/non-gold entities\n- 但转化为下游 F1 时，ΔF1 极小（最好的 MLP 仅 +0.0011），说明 weighted MV 无法持续超越 standard MV\n- 核心原因：agreement_ratio 是压倒性强特征（LR coef ~1.6），而这本质上就是 MV 的投票频率——分类器学到的主要就是\"同意率高的 entity 更可能正确\"，跟 MV 做的事情一样\n- 结果文件在 `/root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/output/distill_verifier/distill_verifier_results.json`"
3	}
```
